# Roblox Creator Docs: Mindset & Process

All Roblox Creator Docs tips on this theme, grouped by topic, oldest first. Cross-creator synthesis: see [[README]] in roblox-kb.

## [[analytics-metrics]]
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/error-reporting) Get error message hashes by querying the ErrorCount metric broken down by the MessageHash dimension before calling log-details. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides error-reporting|Docs: cloud / guides / error-reporting]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/thumbnails) Judge thumbnails by Qualified Play Through Rate (qualified plays ÷ home page impressions), not impressions alone, and check Average Playtime per qualified play. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production publishing thumbnails|Docs: production / publishing / thumbnails]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-events) Read update stats: Views, Visit rate (visits from notification), Unfollow rate. Watch unfollow rate as a negative signal. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion experience-events|Docs: production / promotion / experience-events]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-events) Read event attribution: check GameJoinContext EventId in PlayerAdded to identify event participants and personalize their experience. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion experience-events|Docs: production / promotion / experience-events]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/event-types) Log economy events only after a successful operation (e.g. after a purchase succeeds, not when attempted), since failed attempts create misleading data. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics event-types|Docs: production / analytics / event-types]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/event-types) Use custom fields to break down values instead of creating separate currencies, e.g. one XP currency with class as Custom Field 1, to conserve currency cardinality. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics event-types|Docs: production / analytics / event-types]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/event-types) Use the View Events tool on Economy, Funnel, or Custom pages to check near real-time event lists while charts take about 24 hours to appear. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics event-types|Docs: production / analytics / event-types]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/event-types) Check your game's error report to catch errors in event tracking. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics event-types|Docs: production / analytics / event-types]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/event-types) Plan for rate limits: AnalyticsService allows 120 + (20 × CCU) requests per minute globally, so scale logging with server population. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics event-types|Docs: production / analytics / event-types]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads) Check Monetization > Ads metrics after 48 hours of ads running to see views, impressions, teleports, and per-ad-unit Robux earned. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production monetization immersive-ads|Docs: production / monetization / immersive-ads]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads) Name ad units distinctly in Studio; metrics graphs per ad unit use the Studio instance name. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production monetization immersive-ads|Docs: production / monetization / immersive-ads]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/monetization) Open Creator Dashboard > Creations > your game > Monetization > Overview to see revenue, conversion, paying users, ARPPU and ARPDAU. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics monetization|Docs: production / analytics / monetization]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/first-experience) Use the Creator Dashboard analytics for player activity and retention to decide which iterations to make after publishing. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs tutorials first-experience|Docs: tutorials / first-experience]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts/metrics) Set alerts on client crash rate (ClientCrashRate15m) and unexpected OOM exits (OomUnexpectedExits) to catch stability regressions after releases. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides alerts metrics|Docs: cloud / guides / alerts / metrics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts/metrics) Use percentile metrics (P10/P50/P90) for client FPS and memory, not just averages, to see how low-end devices perform. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides alerts metrics|Docs: cloud / guides / alerts / metrics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts/metrics) Alert on DataStore quota consumption (read/write/list/remove) per DataStoreRequestSource to spot runaway save loops before hitting limits. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides alerts metrics|Docs: cloud / guides / alerts / metrics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts/metrics) Filter alerts by Place to scope them to a single experience; Place is filter-only and cannot be used as a split dimension. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides alerts metrics|Docs: cloud / guides / alerts / metrics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts/metrics) Use Session time percentiles (P10/P50/P90) alongside peak concurrent players to read engagement by platform and OS. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides alerts metrics|Docs: cloud / guides / alerts / metrics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics) Create an API key with universe.analytics:read on universe-analytics and add your experience under Access Permissions; include it as x-api-key on every request. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics) Standard metrics (engagement, monetization, acquisition, retention) have up to 4 years of history; performance metrics only 28 days. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics) Date ranges over two years only support OneWeek, OneMonth, or None granularity. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics) Use granularity None with breakdown and limit to rank top-N series, then run a second query filtered to those values for a display metric. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics) Funnel steps only appear on days when a user reached them; use a 90-day lookback to discover all steps. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics) Weekly and monthly buckets count distinct users across the period, not a sum of daily values. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics) Check data point status: Projected values may change; NotStatisticallySignificant values are unreliable (e.g. crash rate in small countries). ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics) On 429 (data point budget exceeded), shorten the date range, use coarser granularity, or reduce breakdown series with limit. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics) Buckets are UTC-aligned; convert your reporting day to UTC when comparing against dashboard figures. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/learn-explore-sort) Maintain a like ratio above 50% and at least 15 concurrent users to be eligible. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs creator-programs learn-explore-sort|Docs: creator-programs / learn-explore-sort]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-notifications) Use a new notification string per variant to A/B test; the Analytics tab tracks each string as a separate row. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion experience-notifications|Docs: production / promotion / experience-notifica]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-notifications) Stats show only after 100 aggregate impressions; per-notification rows appear once they reach 100 impressions. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion experience-notifications|Docs: production / promotion / experience-notifica]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/share-links) Use the Acquisition page to see new users arriving at the game details page via share links and which channel brings the most engaged users. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion share-links|Docs: production / promotion / share-links]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/analytics-dashboard) Dashboard KPIs unlock only after the game has more than 10 DAU and 10 play hours for 7 consecutive days. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics analytics-dashboard|Docs: production / analytics / analytics-dashboard]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/analytics-dashboard) Use Filter By cohorts (in-experience activity status, user engagement) to separate Early, Active, Lapsed and Reactivated players when reading charts. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics analytics-dashboard|Docs: production / analytics / analytics-dashboard]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/analytics-dashboard) Use period-over-period overlays (e.g. 28-day offset) and 7-day moving averages to remove weekly seasonality before judging trends. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics analytics-dashboard|Docs: production / analytics / analytics-dashboard]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/analytics-dashboard) Compare KPIs against benchmarks; read the 50th-90th percentile range as the spread of similar games, not a single target. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics analytics-dashboard|Docs: production / analytics / analytics-dashboard]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/analytics-dashboard) Create calculated metrics (e.g. RoundWins / RoundsPlayed, CurrencySources - CurrencySinks) to track difficulty, inflation and feature adoption. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics analytics-dashboard|Docs: production / analytics / analytics-dashboard]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/observability) Open the Memory Stores dashboard via Creations > game > Monitoring > Memory Stores; you need game owner or analytics group permission. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud-services memory-stores observability|Docs: cloud-services / memory-stores / observabili]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/observability) Act on a yellow warning once memory passes 70% of quota within an hour, before it turns into a critical overage. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud-services memory-stores observability|Docs: cloud-services / memory-stores / observabili]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/observability) Use the Request Count by Status and Request by API x Status charts to find the ratio of Success to error responses when troubleshooting. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud-services memory-stores observability|Docs: cloud-services / memory-stores / observabili]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/observability) Dashboard data covers only the past 30 days; earlier custom ranges return a Request Failed error, so export or record older data in time. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud-services memory-stores observability|Docs: cloud-services / memory-stores / observabili]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks) Use the Benchmarks API to compare your metrics against genre, similarity, or top-experience groups rather than judging numbers in isolation. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics benchmarks|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics / benchmarks]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks) Set endTime one day later in Analytics Query API than in Benchmarks API, since Benchmarks endTime is inclusive and Analytics endTime is exclusive. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics benchmarks|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics / benchmarks]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks) Request BENCHMARK_TYPE_SIMILARITY explicitly for metrics that only have similarity benchmarks, otherwise the series come back empty. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics benchmarks|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics / benchmarks]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks) If the requested benchmark type has no data, read availableTypes in the response and retry with a supported type. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics benchmarks|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics / benchmarks]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks) Start and end times must be RFC 3339 midnight UTC; a start time later in the day excludes that day's benchmark value. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics benchmarks|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics / benchmarks]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks) Benchmarks API returns 400 for benchmarkVariantId on metrics that don't support l7_average; omit it for those metrics. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics benchmarks|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics / benchmarks]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks) Top-experience benchmarks ignore the percentiles field and return one percentile chosen from your 30-day average value. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics benchmarks|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics / benchmarks]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks) Keep date ranges within 400 days and at most 10 percentiles per request to avoid 400 errors. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics benchmarks|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics / benchmarks]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks) On 429 rate-limit responses, wait and retry at a lower request rate. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics benchmarks|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics / benchmarks]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks) Generate the API key with your experience in Access Permissions and grant universe.analytics:read on universe-analytics, then send it in the x-api-key header. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics benchmarks|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics / benchmarks]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/passes) Use pass analytics to view top passes, sales and net revenue trends, and how many buyers came via the Buy Robux page and joined the game. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production monetization passes|Docs: production / monetization / passes]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/avatar-items) Use Monetization > Avatar Items > Analytics to compare top selling and top grossing items, net revenue, and buyer age, gender, location and platform. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production monetization avatar-items|Docs: production / monetization / avatar-items]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/liveops-planning) Track currency and resource sources and sinks and instrument features so you can see how players interact with them. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design liveops-planning|Docs: production / game-design / liveops-planning]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/liveops-planning) Monitor event data hourly and same-day to catch too many prizes or events players can't complete. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design liveops-planning|Docs: production / game-design / liveops-planning]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/liveops-planning) Compare event-period data with the week before and the week after to see whether the event had a lasting effect. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design liveops-planning|Docs: production / game-design / liveops-planning]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/liveops-planning) Judge event success by retention during and after the event and by item and currency sales during and after. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design liveops-planning|Docs: production / game-design / liveops-planning]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/engagement) Use the New User First Session Retention chart (percent still playing after X minutes, week-on-week) to catch onboarding issues. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics engagement|Docs: production / analytics / engagement]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/engagement) After a drop in new-user retention, check recent updates for new onboarding steps or changed intro messaging, and check error/performance reports. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics engagement|Docs: production / analytics / engagement]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-fields) Use custom fields to slice economy, funnel and custom events by game-specific dimensions such as level, class or weapon type. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics custom-fields|Docs: production / analytics / custom-fields]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-fields) Pass custom field values as strings; convert booleans and numbers to string format before logging. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics custom-fields|Docs: production / analytics / custom-fields]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-fields) Only Enum.AnalyticsCustomFieldKeys.CustomField01/02/03 keys are read; any other keys are silently ignored. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics custom-fields|Docs: production / analytics / custom-fields]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-fields) Prefix values with the dimension name (e.g. "Level - 10") to make charts easier to read later. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics custom-fields|Docs: production / analytics / custom-fields]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-fields) Keep combined custom field values under 8,000 unique combinations across the three fields to avoid unwieldy data. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics custom-fields|Docs: production / analytics / custom-fields]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/developer-console) Use the Developer Console's Network tab to inspect web calls, including DataStore and HttpService requests, with status codes, timing and failures. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs studio developer-console|Docs: studio / developer-console]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/feedback) Use the Feedback chart view to spot sudden downvote spikes after an update, then compare sentiment before and after changes to judge their effect. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics feedback|Docs: production / analytics / feedback]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/feedback) Use the Date Range selector (7/30/60/90 days or custom) and check the relative percentage, which compares the range against the prior equal-length period. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics feedback|Docs: production / analytics / feedback]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/feedback) Export feedback to CSV from the export button for processing in external tools. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics feedback|Docs: production / analytics / feedback]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report) Check the Error Report before and after each update to catch regressions early. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics error-report|Docs: production / analytics / error-report]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report) When a spike appears on the chart, use the error table to troubleshoot, then expand rows for stack traces. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics error-report|Docs: production / analytics / error-report]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report) Use the 'New errors since' filter to isolate errors introduced by your most recent version. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics error-report|Docs: production / analytics / error-report]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report) Use platform and OS breakdowns to see whether an error is specific to Desktop, Mobile, Console, or a given OS. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics error-report|Docs: production / analytics / error-report]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-events) Track custom events for metrics default analytics miss, such as UI adoption, ability usage per map, or kill/death ratios per weapon. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics custom-events|Docs: production / analytics / custom-events]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-events) Send custom events only from the server via AnalyticsService:LogCustomEvent; events can't be sent from the client or Studio. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics custom-events|Docs: production / analytics / custom-events]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-events) Use custom fields instead of many event names (e.g. one PlantSeed event with plant-type field) because event name cardinality is much tighter. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics custom-events|Docs: production / analytics / custom-events]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-events) Batch events with values (e.g. 10 zombies killed as one event) to stay under rate limits. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics custom-events|Docs: production / analytics / custom-events]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-events) Instrument the full core loop (seeds planted, plants watered, shop visits), not just the final action, to find where players drop off or prefer options. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics custom-events|Docs: production / analytics / custom-events]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-events) Expect up to 24 hours before Explore page charts populate, since events are aggregated daily. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics custom-events|Docs: production / analytics / custom-events]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started) Low D1 retention usually signals poor onboarding: players are confused, frustrated, or not reaching the fun fast enough. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics get-started|Docs: production / analytics / get-started]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started) Low D7 retention often means weak progression; players lack a tangible reason to return a week later. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics get-started|Docs: production / analytics / get-started]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started) Low D30 retention often indicates missing end-game content or end-game goals for seasoned players. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics get-started|Docs: production / analytics / get-started]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started) Conversion rate (share of users who ever spend) is one of the most important monetization metrics, since initial purchasers tend to buy again. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics get-started|Docs: production / analytics / get-started]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started) Low ARPPU often means purchasable item value is not optimized; top payers typically spend several times more than other segments. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics get-started|Docs: production / analytics / get-started]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started) Compare average session length against your content length after updates or events to gauge how new content was received. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics get-started|Docs: production / analytics / get-started]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started) Segment by Active Payer Status: if the top 15% of payers contribute much less than ~50% of revenue, improve retention/monetization for top spenders. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics get-started|Docs: production / analytics / get-started]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started) Segment by When user first played: in mature games, older cohorts should make up a growing share of DAU; declining old cohorts call for new content or events. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics get-started|Docs: production / analytics / get-started]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started) Follow the loop: analyze metrics, make a targeted change via configs or experiments, monitor results, then repeat; change incrementally. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics get-started|Docs: production / analytics / get-started]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/funnel-events) Start funnel tracking with the onboarding flow; it is likely where most users are lost. Log a step after each player completes it. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics funnel-events|Docs: production / analytics / funnel-events]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/funnel-events) Be consistent about when you call LogFunnelStepEvent and use easy-to-interpret step names; call after the player completes the step. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics funnel-events|Docs: production / analytics / funnel-events]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/funnel-events) Funnel events can only be sent from the server in published games; they cannot be sent from the client or Studio. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics funnel-events|Docs: production / analytics / funnel-events]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/funnel-events) Use a GUID funnelSessionId for recurring funnels like shop opens within one session; use a item-based key for longer upgrade-path funnels. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics funnel-events|Docs: production / analytics / funnel-events]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/funnel-events) Validate step numbers received from clients on the server against max step before logging, to keep funnel data clean from exploiters. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics funnel-events|Docs: production / analytics / funnel-events]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/funnel-events) After editing funnel steps, set the dashboard date range to start at the edit date to see the updated funnel; mixed ranges show a warning. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics funnel-events|Docs: production / analytics / funnel-events]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/funnel-events) Use custom fields on funnels to compare segments, e.g. starter car choice or map, to find which variant gives better progression. - 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming/slim) Use Developer Console DebugVisualizations > SlimTintMode (LOD or DataModelState) to verify SLIM status; Studio also offers TranscoderStatus while editing. - 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/subscriptions) Track new, renewed, resurrected subscribers and cancellations in the Creator Dashboard Analytics tab; use webhooks for real-time cancelled, purchased, refunded and renewed events. - 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/matchmaking/analytics) Use the Analytics tab date range (previous day to last 90 days) to compare matchmaking performance across periods. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs matchmaking analytics|Docs: matchmaking / analytics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/matchmaking/analytics) For metrics where lower is better (e.g. Estimate Ping, Age Difference), read P10 as best case and P90 as worst case. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs matchmaking analytics|Docs: matchmaking / analytics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/matchmaking/analytics) Switch the Filter By menu between average, P10, P50 and P90 to avoid being misled by averages skewed by outliers. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs matchmaking analytics|Docs: matchmaking / analytics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/acquisition) Open Creator Dashboard > your game > Analytics > Acquisition to see new vs returning users by source and the cumulative new-user funnel. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics acquisition|Docs: production / analytics / acquisition]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/acquisition) Create share links per channel and compare Users with qualified plays, 7D playtime, D7 retention and 30D revenue per user in the New users funnel table. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics acquisition|Docs: production / analytics / acquisition]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/acquisition) Run an acquisition funnel to find sources that drive the most new users and sources with the best end-to-end conversion rate. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics acquisition|Docs: production / analytics / acquisition]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/extended-services) Watch for email alerts at 80% and 100% of budget; decreasing budget below month-to-date usage throttles the service. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud-services extended-services|Docs: cloud-services / extended-services]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/reporting-and-billing) Read ad performance at three levels: campaign for budget allocation, ad set for targeting and budget effectiveness, individual ad for creative impact. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion reporting-and-billing|Docs: production / promotion / reporting-and-billi]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/reporting-and-billing) Track CPP (total cost divided by plays) alongside CTR and play rate; play rate is plays divided by impressions. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion reporting-and-billing|Docs: production / promotion / reporting-and-billi]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/reporting-and-billing) Use 7D playtime and 30D Robux earnings per converted player to judge ad traffic quality beyond raw clicks. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion reporting-and-billing|Docs: production / promotion / reporting-and-billi]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/obby) Track opt-in rate (eligible users who engage with the ad, DUV/DAU) and daily frequency (views per user) as the two key earnings metrics. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion rewarded-video-playbooks obby|Docs: production / promotion / rewarded-video-play]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/tools) Track engagement, retention, and monetization KPIs in the Creator Dashboard, which is free of charge. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs get-started tools|Docs: get-started / tools]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/modes) Pin Counters mode graphs to watch min and max values of instance count and memory usage over time. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs performance-optimization microprofiler modes|Docs: performance-optimization / microprofiler / m]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/onboarding-techniques) Track whether players who haven't used a feature (e.g. trade) after two sessions need a timed hint to surface it. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design onboarding-techniques|Docs: production / game-design / onboarding-techni]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/onboarding-techniques) A/B test hint timing with Experiments (e.g. 5s vs 10s) measuring funnel completion, then push the winner globally via Configs without restarting servers. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design onboarding-techniques|Docs: production / game-design / onboarding-techni]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/experiments) Read operations need universe:read scope; write operations need universe:write, so issue keys with least privilege. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides experiments|Docs: cloud / guides / experiments]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/why-build-on-roblox) Use built-in Roblox analytics for retention, engagement and monetization KPIs; they are free and need no setup. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs get-started why-build-on-roblox|Docs: get-started / why-build-on-roblox]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/shooter) Tag each rewarded video surface with a Placement ID to see which placement drives the most views. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion rewarded-video-playbooks shooter|Docs: production / promotion / rewarded-video-play]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization) Run the cycle: identify issues with built-in tools, improve, then monitor impact with analytics tools. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs performance-optimization|Docs: performance-optimization]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/matchmaking) Use matchmaking analytics to track how well each configuration performs and to find optimisation opportunities. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs matchmaking|Docs: matchmaking]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/identify) Use the Performance Dashboard to track client/server memory, client FPS, server heartbeat and crash rates over time in live sessions. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs performance-optimization identify|Docs: performance-optimization / identify]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/overview) Use Attributes in the Properties window to let team members tweak values at runtime without touching code. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs studio overview|Docs: studio / overview]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/scripting/record-and-display-player-data) Create leaderstats as a Folder under each Player with IntValue/NumberValue/StringValue children so Roblox automatically renders the default leaderboard UI. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs tutorials curriculums core scripting record-and-display-player-da|Docs: tutorials / curriculums / core / scripting /]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/best-practices) Monitor quota usage, request volume and response statuses in the Memory Store Observability Dashboard; set custom alerts and use the Error Report. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud-services memory-stores best-practices|Docs: cloud-services / memory-stores / best-practi]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments) Compute minimum detectable effect (MDE) before running; if MDE exceeds ~100% for your goal metric, significance is unlikely. Games under 1,000 DAU may struggle to get useful data. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production experiments|Docs: production / experiments]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments) Let experiments run their full 14-60 day duration; ending early risks acting on novelty-effect spikes. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production experiments|Docs: production / experiments]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments) Only act on statistically significant results, i.e. where the confidence interval for percent change does not overlap 0%. Ignore non-significant changes. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production experiments|Docs: production / experiments]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments) Write a cause-and-effect hypothesis (change, expected effect, reason) before each experiment and keep them with results. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production experiments|Docs: production / experiments]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments) Stop and restart an experiment if a Sample Ratio Mismatch banner appears; SRM can invalidate results. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production experiments|Docs: production / experiments]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments) Do not use first-24-hour early-harm metrics for launch decisions; use them only to guide early stopping. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production experiments|Docs: production / experiments]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments) Use confidence intervals for deep dives and borderline significance; very wide intervals may never reach significance. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production experiments|Docs: production / experiments]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments) For matchmaking experiments, use 100% rollout; it minimizes risk of isolating players and gives faster, more reliable results. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production experiments|Docs: production / experiments]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/video-service) Check the Video Service dashboard (Creator Dashboard > Monitoring) to compare efficient vs inefficient video usage after adding or changing videos. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud-services video-service|Docs: cloud-services / video-service]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/observability) Open Creations > Monitoring > Data Stores in Creator Hub to watch DataStore request counts and quota usage; you need owner or analytics group permission. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud-services data-stores observability|Docs: cloud-services / data-stores / observability]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/observability) Use Request Count by Status and Request by API x Status to find which DataStore API methods are returning errors before players notice data issues. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud-services data-stores observability|Docs: cloud-services / data-stores / observability]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/observability) Read Write List Remove Quota Usage charts show usage against future per-category limits; check them before launch and growth to avoid throttling. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud-services data-stores observability|Docs: cloud-services / data-stores / observability]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/observability) Dashboard data from the last 3 minutes may be incomplete, so ignore the drop at the chart end. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud-services data-stores observability|Docs: cloud-services / data-stores / observability]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/device-trust) Before enforcing a policy, check the Anti-cheat page under Safety in Creator Hub. It estimates DAU, playtime and revenue by trust level on supported platforms. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs scripting security device-trust|Docs: scripting / security / device-trust]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/recommendation) Pass the itemId returned by RegisterItemAsync or GenerateItemListAsync to LogImpressionEvent, not a raw referenceId string. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production recommendation|Docs: production / recommendation]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/recommendation) For static cards without video, set impression Duration to 1 to get clean 'user viewed this item' data. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production recommendation|Docs: production / recommendation]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/recommendation) Only log play actions when a user passes a satisfaction threshold (e.g. over 60 seconds), not on every Play click. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production recommendation|Docs: production / recommendation]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/recommendation) Use Enum.RecommendationActionType.Play for play-session actions; other enums won't register correctly with MaximizePlays. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production recommendation|Docs: production / recommendation]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/recommendation) Never log plays from Search, random picks, or past plays as recommendation actions, and never use dummy tracing IDs; the model expects realistic impression-to-play ratios. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production recommendation|Docs: production / recommendation]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/recommendation) Track Total Actions, Total Unique Users, Average Items Impressed per User, and Average Time Spent per User in Creator Hub Recommendation Service analytics. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production recommendation|Docs: production / recommendation]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/RTBF-and-creators) Check your inbox daily for Roblox RTBF messages, which list pending requests requiring action and the affected games. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production publishing RTBF-and-creators|Docs: production / publishing / RTBF-and-creators]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/regional-pricing) Use the Sales page's Top Countries/Regions list to see adjusted regional prices per pass or product. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production monetization regional-pricing|Docs: production / monetization / regional-pricing]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/sponsor-items) Track CTR (clicks/impressions), CPC, conversion rate (attributed purchases/impressions) and CPP (Robux spent/attributed purchases) to judge sponsored item performance. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs marketplace sponsor-items|Docs: marketplace / sponsor-items]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/optimization/scriptprofiler) Export profiling data as JSON to compare sessions or analyze node and function TotalDuration values outside Studio. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs studio optimization scriptprofiler|Docs: studio / optimization / scriptprofiler]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/retention) Read retention by cohort: x-axis is each user's first play date, so changes made on a given day show up in that day's new-user cohort. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics retention|Docs: production / analytics / retention]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/retention) Expect D7 and D30 data to be empty for recent dates; D7 appears after 7 days and D30 after 30 days. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics retention|Docs: production / analytics / retention]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/retention) Use daily and weekly cohort tables to compare users acquired during events or campaigns against earlier users, including 7D playtime, 7D conversion, and 7D/30D revenue per user. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics retention|Docs: production / analytics / retention]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/retention) Measure progression by granting a special item at each level to see how long players take to reach the next level, then adjust task difficulty. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics retention|Docs: production / analytics / retention]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players/leaderboards) Set stat values through .Value (e.g. gold.Value), not by assigning the object directly; stats must be IntValue/NumberValue/StringValue children of leaderstats. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs players leaderboards|Docs: players / leaderboards]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alert/metrics) Set alerts on client crash rate (ClientCrashRate15m) and OOM exits by platform and memory group to catch device-specific stability problems early. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides alert metrics|Docs: cloud / guides / alert / metrics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alert/metrics) Use percentile metrics (P10/P50/P90) for frame rate and memory, not just averages, to see how low-end players experience the game. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides alert metrics|Docs: cloud / guides / alert / metrics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alert/metrics) Use OneMinute granularity for live incident alerts; the longer granularities suit trend monitoring. Data is kept for 28 days only. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides alert metrics|Docs: cloud / guides / alert / metrics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery-faq) Check Creator Analytics > Overview and Acquisition > Home Recommendations to see where your game is strong and where to focus. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs discovery-faq|Docs: discovery-faq]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/metrics) Query retention by segment dimensions (Platform, Country, AcquisitionSource, UserSegmentationPayerStatus) rather than only the blended D1/D7/D30 number. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics metrics|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics / metrics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/metrics) Export performance metrics within 28 days; client and server performance data is only retained for 28 days. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics metrics|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics / metrics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/metrics) Export thumbnail metrics within 30 days; thumbnail data is only retained for 30 days. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics metrics|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics / metrics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/metrics) Use ClientFpsP10 rather than the average to see the experience of your slowest players; P10 captures low-end performance. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics metrics|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics / metrics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/metrics) Segment by IsNewUser when reading engagement metrics so new-player behaviour is not diluted by returning players. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics metrics|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics / metrics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/performance) Monitor the Performance dashboard before and after every update to catch regressions early. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics performance|Docs: production / analytics / performance]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/performance) Dashboard charts require at least 100 daily active users (DAU) to display. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics performance|Docs: production / analytics / performance]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/performance) Use Breakdown by Place Version to compare the latest release against earlier versions for lower FPS or more out-of-memory exits. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics performance|Docs: production / analytics / performance]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players/users) Group in-game checks (IsInGroupAsync, GetRankInGroupAsync, GetRoleInGroupAsync, PromptJoinAsync) need no user ID handling; Open Cloud group APIs use the group's domain IDs. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs players users|Docs: players / users]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/liveops-essentials) Use analytics and the creator dashboard's performance data and suggestions to find issues and opportunities to target with updates. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design liveops-essentials|Docs: production / game-design / liveops-essential]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/planning-for-educational-settings) Capture completion, timestamps, performance, trajectory and GUI interaction data in-game to show educators student progress; comply with Roblox privacy rules. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education developer planning-for-educational-settings|Docs: education / developer / planning-for-educati]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/planning-for-educational-settings) Use short-response or fill-in-the-blank assessments over multiple choice, which the docs caution is too simple to show real understanding. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education developer planning-for-educational-settings|Docs: education / developer / planning-for-educati]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/demographics) Use Demographics (Creator Dashboard > Analytics) to prioritize future content updates based on your MAU's age, country and language mix. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics demographics|Docs: production / analytics / demographics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/demographics) Look for audience segments you aren't yet serving and use them to attract new audiences. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics demographics|Docs: production / analytics / demographics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/demographics) Group-owned games require group analytics permissions to view demographics. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics demographics|Docs: production / analytics / demographics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/onboarding) Test different starting currency amounts across new-player groups with Experiments to find the value that maximises retention, then set it globally with Configs. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design onboarding|Docs: production / game-design / onboarding]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/content-updates) Use player behaviour analytics and feedback to pick which content types players regularly want most. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design content-updates|Docs: production / game-design / content-updates]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/http-service) Use the Observability Dashboard to track request count and response time per request type and status to diagnose failing endpoints. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud-services http-service|Docs: cloud-services / http-service]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts) Set consecutiveOccurrences so a spike must persist before firing; OneMinute interval allows 6–30, other intervals allow 1–10. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides alerts|Docs: cloud / guides / alerts]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts) Use PeriodOverPeriod with periodOffsetMultiplier 7 on OneDay interval to compare each day with the same day last week. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides alerts|Docs: cloud / guides / alerts]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts) Any dimension used in a breakdown must also appear in filter; only one breakdown dimension is allowed per alert. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides alerts|Docs: cloud / guides / alerts]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts) Keep to 20 alerts per experience; delete unused alerts before creating new ones when hitting MAX_ALERT_REACHED. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides alerts|Docs: cloud / guides / alerts]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts) Query incident history with startTime within last 30 days; incidents are retained about 35 days. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides alerts|Docs: cloud / guides / alerts]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/test-on-hardware) Use the Performance Dashboard to track FPS, memory and crash rates by device category after publish; a crash spike after an update suggests a low-end memory regression. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs performance-optimization test-on-hardware|Docs: performance-optimization / test-on-hardware]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/alerts) Establish a baseline first: review analytics charts over the last 7 to 14 days before setting thresholds, so normal fluctuation does not trigger false alarms. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics alerts|Docs: production / analytics / alerts]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/alerts) Set alert thresholds above normal levels. Example: if normal crash rate is about 1.2%, set the alert at 2.5% or 3%. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics alerts|Docs: production / analytics / alerts]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/alerts) Use a duration of 5 (not 1) for volatile metrics like session time; sudden player influxes or asset loads can cause temporary spikes. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics alerts|Docs: production / analytics / alerts]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/alerts) Use the Breakdown feature in one alert (e.g. by platform: Android, iOS, PC) instead of separate alerts per platform; the payload shows where the issue occurs. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics alerts|Docs: production / analytics / alerts]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/alerts) Name alerts with a structured formula like 'metric - context' (e.g. 'High client crash rate - mobile') so issues can be triaged at a glance. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics alerts|Docs: production / analytics / alerts]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads) Estimate earnings with the Eligibility calculator: eligible users (7-day) x ads per user x projected EPM. Daily ad views per user is the main lever you control. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion rewarded-video-ads|Docs: production / promotion / rewarded-video-ads]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads) Track Opt-In Rate, DUV/eDAU, Frequency, AEPDUV, and EPM under Monetization > Ads > Analytics to judge rewarded video performance. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion rewarded-video-ads|Docs: production / promotion / rewarded-video-ads]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads) Use experiments to roll a new ad placement out to a percentage of users first and measure engagement and retention before full rollout. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion rewarded-video-ads|Docs: production / promotion / rewarded-video-ads]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/engagement-based-payouts) Use Premium Playtime Score (daily Premium engagement time) for immediate feedback on new features; Premium Playtime Robux Earned aggregates 28 days, so it lags. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production monetization engagement-based-payouts|Docs: production / monetization / engagement-based]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/economy-events) Log economy events only from the server; events sent from the client or Studio are not tracked. Games must be published. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics economy-events|Docs: production / analytics / economy-events]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/economy-events) Always pass the cost/amount as a positive number; the dashboard renders sinks as negative automatically. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics economy-events|Docs: production / analytics / economy-events]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/economy-events) Include an itemSKU on source and sink events; without it the sources and sinks table shows N/A. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics economy-events|Docs: production / analytics / economy-events]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/economy-events) Track every place users gain (sources) and spend (sinks) a currency so the Economy dashboard reflects the real economy. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics economy-events|Docs: production / analytics / economy-events]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/economy-events) Use custom fields (e.g. quest name, store location) to break down which segments earn or spend the most. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics economy-events|Docs: production / analytics / economy-events]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/economy-events) Use the default transaction types (IAP, TimedReward, Onboarding, Shop, Gameplay, ContextualPurchase) first; custom type names are optional. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics economy-events|Docs: production / analytics / economy-events]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/analytics-essentials) Start with the three KPIs (engagement, retention, monetization) to find the highest-level areas for improvement in your game's health. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design analytics-essentials|Docs: production / game-design / analytics-essenti]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/insights) Use the 'Explore this metric' button on an overview insight to slice revenue or other metrics by platform, country, and more. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics insights|Docs: production / analytics / insights]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/insights) Compare your metrics against similar-game benchmarks, not just your own history; a metric can improve yet still lag benchmarks and needs continued work. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics insights|Docs: production / analytics / insights]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/insights) Check the weekly and monthly AI analytics reports to find which monetization product or acquisition channel drives revenue and DAU changes. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics insights|Docs: production / analytics / insights]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/insights) Use automatic outlier detection in reports to find segments (platform, age, country) behind a metric decline instead of filtering manually. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics insights|Docs: production / analytics / insights]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/crashes) Watch the server crashes chart for out-of-memory spikes; filter by place version to pin the release that introduced a memory leak. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics crashes|Docs: production / analytics / crashes]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/crashes) Open a server out-of-memory snapshot in the treemap viewer and drill into nodes to see which services, folders or assets dominated memory at crash time. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics crashes|Docs: production / analytics / crashes]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/crashes) Download the raw CSV of memory snapshots for custom analysis, or parse it with a script or LLM. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics crashes|Docs: production / analytics / crashes]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/developer-awards) Monitor MAU, 12-month rolling Robux earned, and lifetime play hours; these are the metrics Roblox uses to recognise developer milestones. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs creator-programs developer-awards|Docs: creator-programs / developer-awards]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/tycoon) Focus on two metrics: opt-in rate (target 15%+ of eligible users engaging) and daily frequency (target 3+ views per user per day). ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion rewarded-video-playbooks tycoon|Docs: production / promotion / rewarded-video-play]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-dashboards) Group dashboards by purpose, e.g. Economy Health, Live-Ops, or Retention, so related charts sit in one view. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics custom-dashboards|Docs: production / analytics / custom-dashboards]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-dashboards) Put top KPIs such as daily revenue, D1 retention, or peak CCU in summary cards at the top of each dashboard. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics custom-dashboards|Docs: production / analytics / custom-dashboards]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-dashboards) Click Publish after editing a dashboard; unpublished changes are lost when you leave the page. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics custom-dashboards|Docs: production / analytics / custom-dashboards]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-dashboards) Pin daily-check dashboards to the left navigation with Pin to sidebar for one-click access. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics custom-dashboards|Docs: production / analytics / custom-dashboards]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-dashboards) Set summary card aggregation to average, most recent value, or cumulative depending on whether the KPI is a rate, a current level, or a total. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics custom-dashboards|Docs: production / analytics / custom-dashboards]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/experience-notifications) Set analytics_data category per notification type to segment and compare performance across notification categories. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides experience-notifications|Docs: cloud / guides / experience-notifications]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/monitor) Use the Performance Dashboard client and server tabs with date ranges to track how each update affected performance over time. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs performance-optimization monitor|Docs: performance-optimization / monitor]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/monitor) Compare the Performance Dashboard with other analytics dashboards to link performance changes to business metrics like session time. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs performance-optimization monitor|Docs: performance-optimization / monitor]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies) Define KPIs (engagement, retention, monetization) before development begins so they guide design from the start. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs get-started strategies|Docs: get-started / strategies]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising) Read campaign spend, impressions and plays through the Analytics API, not the Ads endpoints. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides advertising|Docs: cloud / guides / advertising]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics) Before driving many new users, get D1 retention and average session time to benchmark level or above similar games. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics|Docs: production / analytics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics) Use benchmark scorecards (available at 100+ DAU) to find the weakest metric versus similar games, then use funnel analytics on onboarding. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics|Docs: production / analytics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences) Track onboarding and tutorial completion, drop-off points, D1/D7 retention, session length, and age demographics during a beta. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education developer beta-testing-experiences|Docs: education / developer / beta-testing-experie]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences) Check that the beta's actual age mix matches your intended target age; a mismatch signals a design or marketing problem. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education developer beta-testing-experiences|Docs: education / developer / beta-testing-experie]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences) Aim for at least an 80% like ratio after a beta closes; likes strongly influence whether new players try the game. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education developer beta-testing-experiences|Docs: education / developer / beta-testing-experie]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/balance-virtual-economies) Track who buys what, what actions yield currency, earned vs purchased currency use, item usage timing, and feature interaction frequency. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design balance-virtual-economies|Docs: production / game-design / balance-virtual-e]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/balance-virtual-economies) Use data visualization after events; a Gold surplus with flat purchases signals unbalanced earning and lower post-event revenue. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design balance-virtual-economies|Docs: production / game-design / balance-virtual-e]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager) Compare performance using Recent Users or Resurrected Users views; All Users can look low for retention, plays, or Robux revenue. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion ads-manager|Docs: production / promotion / ads-manager]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager) Read Earnings ROAS as USD earnings attributed divided by spend; wait for Estimated ROAS to finalize as attribution windows close. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion ads-manager|Docs: production / promotion / ads-manager]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/localization) Download monthly translation analytics to track translation coverage and translator contributions. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production localization|Docs: production / localization]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/best-practices) Monitor requests, response status, throughput and storage in the Data Stores Observability Dashboard and set custom alerts for sustained failures or unexpected growth. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud-services data-stores best-practices|Docs: cloud-services / data-stores / best-practice]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/best-practices) Use Data Stores Manager to inspect stores, keys, storage and estimated costs. With more than 100 data stores, use Open Cloud or the Data Stores Batch Processor for size and key counts. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud-services data-stores best-practices|Docs: cloud-services / data-stores / best-practice]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery) Check play-through rate, play days and playtime first in Analytics > Acquisition > Home Recommendations, then secondary signals. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs discovery|Docs: discovery]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/ui-ux-design) Use Experiments to test button placements and HUD layouts against player engagement; use Configs to toggle platform-specific UI or seasonal themes across servers. ## [[playtesting-and-feedback]]
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs) Use conditional configs with experiments to give different values to player segments and compare outcomes. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production configs|Docs: production / configs]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/finishing-the-project) Test every number yourself and have friends playtest; initial values are educated guesses, not final balance. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education adventure-game-series finishing-the-project|Docs: education / adventure-game-series / finishin]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/finishing-the-project) During playtests, check whether items are too easy or too far to find, whether upgrade cost feels too easy or hard, and look for unnoticed bugs. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education adventure-game-series finishing-the-project|Docs: education / adventure-game-series / finishin]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/prototyping) Run several playtest sessions with your team to confirm findings, then share the prototype with friends, family and social media for fresh feedback. - 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/classic-clothing) Test classic clothing in Studio on a Block Avatar Rig before uploading, to confirm it looks as intended; testing is free. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs avatar classic-clothing|Docs: avatar / classic-clothing]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/coding/next-steps) Invite early testers to preview your game before release, using the Invite early testers docs, so others can play it while it is still private. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs tutorials curriculums coding next-steps|Docs: tutorials / curriculums / coding / next-step]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/animator/get-started) Playtest the template before editing so you understand the gameplay loop before changing anything. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs tutorials curriculums animator get-started|Docs: tutorials / curriculums / animator / get-sta]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/finishing-the-project) Playtest frequently with peers and check whether match duration feels right and whether the map is too empty to find other players quickly. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education battle-royale-series finishing-the-project|Docs: education / battle-royale-series / finishing]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy/change-the-script) Playtest after each balance change to judge how it feels before changing more values.
