- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines) Test and iterate often on a real Quest 2 headset to confirm performance and visual quality. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production publishing vr-guidelines|Docs: production / publishing / vr-guidelines]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/building/next-steps) Invite early testers to preview your game before releasing it publicly, per the docs' 'Invite early testers' guide. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs tutorials curriculums building next-steps|Docs: tutorials / curriculums / building / next-st]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/galactic-speedway-lesson) Set sportsmanship guidelines for playtesting: respect others, don't make excuses when losing, don't rub it in when winning. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education lesson-plans galactic-speedway-lesson|Docs: education / lesson-plans / galactic-speedway]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/feedback) Read every comment, including on downvotes; players who downvote often leave useful suggestions. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics feedback|Docs: production / analytics / feedback]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/customize-and-share) After changing a tuning value, playtest and judge whether the speed fits the vehicle's design and feels exciting in a race. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway customize-and-share|Docs: education / build-it-play-it-galactic-speedw]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/digital-citizenship/finishing-projects) Ask testers to play only after a self-check pass; the lesson treats self-evaluation as the step before feedback. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education lesson-plans digital-citizenship finishing-projects|Docs: education / lesson-plans / digital-citizensh]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/landing) Test the finished project and iterate on it; the series frames testing as a core design step. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education adventure-game-series landing|Docs: education / adventure-game-series / landing]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/how-is-roblox-different) Ship updates quickly and use the resulting direct player feedback to iterate toward a more focused experience for your audience. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs get-started how-is-roblox-different|Docs: get-started / how-is-roblox-different]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/onboarding-techniques) Set timed-hint delays from playtest data: e.g. if most players finish a step within 10 seconds, show the hint at 11 seconds. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design onboarding-techniques|Docs: production / game-design / onboarding-techni]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/onboarding-techniques) Show each timed hint only once per task, and only after most players have completed it, so experienced players aren't annoyed. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design onboarding-techniques|Docs: production / game-design / onboarding-techni]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/animator/next-steps) Invite early testers to preview your game before releasing it to players. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs tutorials curriculums animator next-steps|Docs: tutorials / curriculums / animator / next-st]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/animate-in-roblox-lesson) Pair students to review each other's animation using the feedback handout, after setting a goal for the animation beforehand. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education lesson-plans animate-in-roblox-lesson|Docs: education / lesson-plans / animate-in-roblox]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/animate-in-roblox-lesson) Limit play-testing to about 2 minutes per check so the lesson stays on schedule. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education lesson-plans animate-in-roblox-lesson|Docs: education / lesson-plans / animate-in-roblox]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer-lesson) Have a peer playtest the game and give feedback before finishing; evaluate against fun, challenging-but-fair, and error/glitch-free goals. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education lesson-plans roblox-developer-lesson|Docs: education / lesson-plans / roblox-developer-]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments) Use a simple 50/50 split of one variant vs control on large rollouts; it is easier to configure and interpret than multi-variant splits. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production experiments|Docs: production / experiments]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/controller-emulator) Watch the device picture during play; if a control doesn't highlight, the problem is upstream of your input handling. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs studio controller-emulator|Docs: studio / controller-emulator]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/landing) Evaluate the finished game against three goals: fun, challenging, and bug-free; redesign or improve as needed before sharing with others. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education lesson-plans roblox-developer landing|Docs: education / lesson-plans / roblox-developer ]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/digital-citizenship/lights-and-polish) Give peer feedback using a structured handout, then have each builder pick one goal from it and implement a change in the next session. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education lesson-plans digital-citizenship lights-and-polish|Docs: education / lesson-plans / digital-citizensh]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/edu-templates/edu-lesson-plan-template) Structure lessons with Introduction and Wrap-up, plus Guided Tutorial, Guided Work, Independent Work, or Activity blocks. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education edu-templates edu-lesson-plan-template|Docs: education / edu-templates / edu-lesson-plan-]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/project-setup) Playtest the graybox and check: can players move without getting stuck, does the size feel right, any floating or misaligned parts. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education battle-royale-series project-setup|Docs: education / battle-royale-series / project-s]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-4) Set a strict playtest time limit, such as one minute, to keep students on track. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education lesson-plans roblox-developer roblox-developer-4|Docs: education / lesson-plans / roblox-developer ]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-4) Give each playtest a specific goal, such as checking whether a tool harvests an item as intended, to stay focused. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education lesson-plans roblox-developer roblox-developer-4|Docs: education / lesson-plans / roblox-developer ]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/digital-citizenship/animating-characters) Get peer feedback using a structured handout that asks for observations and one recommendation; treat feedback as a growth opportunity even if you disagree. - 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/greybox-your-environment) Playtest the layout at nearly every development step to catch issues before they grow costly; check team advantage, orientation, choice overload, and bypassable areas. - 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/paid-access-robux) Use temporary paid access as a closed beta for your most engaged users. - 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/building/test-and-save) Playtest often to confirm the obby is completable; use Test or Test Here, not Run, so your avatar is inserted and you can check jump distances between platforms. - 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-story-games/finish-and-add) Playtest the game every time you add a new set of variables and strings. - 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-story-games/finish-and-add) Ask a peer or friend which other words they would like to customize. - 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects) Use Rapid playtesting, multi-client simulation, device emulation and collaborative playtesting in Studio before releasing to the public. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects|Docs: projects]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/network-simulator) Run a baseline on Ideal Fiber, then repeat the same journey on Home Wi-Fi or Standard Mobile (4G/LTE) and Bad Connection (3G) to isolate network-caused problems. - 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/build-an-obby-lesson) Use peer feedback format: two Stars (what they liked) and one Wish (what they want to see); then implement one change based on feedback. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education lesson-plans build-an-obby-lesson|Docs: education / lesson-plans / build-an-obby-les]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/build-an-obby-lesson) Have students switch seats and play-test each other's game, then allow five minutes to improve based on feedback. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education lesson-plans build-an-obby-lesson|Docs: education / lesson-plans / build-an-obby-les]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-5) Have a friend play the game and collect feedback once the core build is finished. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education lesson-plans roblox-developer roblox-developer-5|Docs: education / lesson-plans / roblox-developer ]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/create-a-score-bar) Test UI in Studio's Device Emulator with Actual Resolution across Phone, Tablet, Desktop and Console presets; the Studio viewport aspect ratio does not match player screens. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs tutorials use-case-tutorials ui create-a-score-bar|Docs: tutorials / use-case-tutorials / ui / create]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/analytics-essentials) Use Experiments to A/B test a specific change so you can measure its causal impact on your metrics rather than guessing. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design analytics-essentials|Docs: production / game-design / analytics-essenti]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/insights) Read the player feedback report for top requests and takeaways, then prioritize the next work from them; verify AI summaries against your own judgment. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics insights|Docs: production / analytics / insights]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/artist/next-steps) Invite early testers to preview an unpublished game before release; a new published game is only playable by its creator until shared. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs tutorials curriculums artist next-steps|Docs: tutorials / curriculums / artist / next-step]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy/playtest-the-map) Run through the full in-game flow (lobby, intermission, map) before calling a build done, to catch bugs and unfun moments. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy playtest-the-map|Docs: education / build-it-play-it-create-and-dest]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/tycoon) Continuously A/B test placement, button design, reward type and reward amount to find the highest-converting combination. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion rewarded-video-playbooks tycoon|Docs: production / promotion / rewarded-video-play]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/artist/test-and-save) Playtest VFX in the real game flow, not just in the Studio viewport, to confirm emitters fire and look correct. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs tutorials curriculums artist test-and-save|Docs: tutorials / curriculums / artist / test-and-]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies) Use small group playtests to check players understand the game and find it fun. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs get-started strategies|Docs: get-started / strategies]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies) Run a limited-time public test (weekend/week) to get organic D1 retention and session length; then remove the game. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs get-started strategies|Docs: get-started / strategies]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies) Compare analytics to target KPIs, find causes via community, change, retest; repeat until KPIs hit targets. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs get-started strategies|Docs: get-started / strategies]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences) Launch a beta only when the game is playable end to end, performs on all target platforms, and you have people dedicated to handling feedback. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education developer beta-testing-experiences|Docs: education / developer / beta-testing-experie]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences) If unsure which beta type to use, start with closed access testing; it is the safest and still surfaces bugs and gameplay issues. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education developer beta-testing-experiences|Docs: education / developer / beta-testing-experie]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences) Use closed access betas via a Roblox group with role-based access to test unpolished features with a trusted, engaged group before going wider. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education developer beta-testing-experiences|Docs: education / developer / beta-testing-experie]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences) Use a limited time test window (e.g. a weekend) to get short-term D1 retention, playtime and session length without a month-long beta commitment. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education developer beta-testing-experiences|Docs: education / developer / beta-testing-experie]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences) In an open access beta, tag the game title with Beta and use in-game pop-ups to set expectations about bugs and unfinished features. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education developer beta-testing-experiences|Docs: education / developer / beta-testing-experie]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences) Design in-game feedback surveys carefully: leading questions skew data, and someone must clean responses before use. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education developer beta-testing-experiences|Docs: education / developer / beta-testing-experie]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences) Run think-aloud sessions with participants from your target age group and use a script to avoid biasing them. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education developer beta-testing-experiences|Docs: education / developer / beta-testing-experie]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/intro-to-game-and-coding) Have students playtest each other's games and give feedback as 2 Stars (what they liked) and a Wish (what they'd want to see). ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education lesson-plans intro-to-game-and-coding|Docs: education / lesson-plans / intro-to-game-and]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/intro-to-game-and-coding) Set strict time limits for tasks, e.g. 1 minute to pick a part color, 2 minutes to playtest, to keep sessions on track. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education lesson-plans intro-to-game-and-coding|Docs: education / lesson-plans / intro-to-game-and]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/ui-ux-design) Meet and play with your target players and learn their goals and preferences to inform design choices. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design ui-ux-design|Docs: production / game-design / ui-ux-design]])

## [[team-and-budget]]
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/collaboration/collaboration-best-practices) Assign team roles (e.g. world builder, coder) and rotate them so members learn new skills. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education collaboration collaboration-best-practices|Docs: education / collaboration / collaboration-be]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/collaboration) Before adding collaborators via Team Create, complete an age check (ID or facial age estimation); group/game owners must do so for collaborators to join. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects collaboration|Docs: projects / collaboration]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/collaboration) Only Roblox friends can be granted Edit permission on a user-owned game; anyone can be granted Play access. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects collaboration|Docs: projects / collaboration]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/collaboration) For group games, grant Edit to specific group roles across all games (e.g., Audio Artist) or per-game for temporary access (e.g., FX Artist before release). ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects collaboration|Docs: projects / collaboration]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/collaboration-best-practices) Assign group roles (e.g. world builder, coder) and rotate them so members learn new skills and discover unexpected interests. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education resources collaboration-best-practices|Docs: education / resources / collaboration-best-p]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/collaboration-best-practices) Resolve team conflicts by discussing interests and goals rather than positions; use 'I feel' messages instead of 'You did' messages. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education resources collaboration-best-practices|Docs: education / resources / collaboration-best-p]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/getting-started-for-developers-education) If you need developers, coders, or creators, find them via the Talent Hub; Roblox does not develop games itself. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education resources getting-started-for-developers-education|Docs: education / resources / getting-started-for-]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/incubator) Incubator targets small, experienced teams; solo beginners are not the stated audience. Plan for a significant development commitment during the program. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs creator-programs incubator|Docs: creator-programs / incubator]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/groups) Assign roles with least-privilege permissions; check each role's permissions before adding members, since permission changes apply only when you click the apply button. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects groups|Docs: projects / groups]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/groups) Use recurring payouts with per-game and group-wide percentage splits; the remainder goes to the group balance, so model the split math before launch. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects groups|Docs: projects / groups]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/game-ownership-transfer) Before transferring, verify your email and move private ModuleScripts and InsertService assets to the target group; user-owned packages may need recreating or replacing with group-owned ones. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects game-ownership-transfer|Docs: projects / game-ownership-transfer]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/external-tools) Consider external tooling mainly for larger teams that need change tracking, code review and familiar languages; use Script Sync if you only need an external editor or version control. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects external-tools|Docs: projects / external-tools]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/assistant/guide) Avoid editing the same place from multiple devices at once; chat history syncs via the cloud and simultaneous work causes conflicts. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs assistant guide|Docs: assistant / guide]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/tools) Use Team Create so multiple users can edit the same project simultaneously, making collaboration with teammates easier. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs get-started tools|Docs: get-started / tools]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/tools) Find and review artists, programmers, and designers on Talent Hub before hiring. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs get-started tools|Docs: get-started / tools]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio) Use group-based access to control team members' permissions, shared assets, and profit splits on a game. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs studio|Docs: studio]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/commerce-products) Group-owned commerce stores require Configure and spend group revenue and Create and edit community experiences permissions; group ownership transfer is blocked while the group owns commerce items. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production monetization commerce-products|Docs: production / monetization / commerce-product]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/configs) Send previousDraftHash on updates and publishes so a stale write fails instead of silently overwriting a teammate's changes; re-read and merge. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides configs|Docs: cloud / guides / configs]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/why-build-on-roblox) You don't need a big team to make a big game; the platform handles hosting, storage, and support so a small team can focus on gameplay. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs get-started why-build-on-roblox|Docs: get-started / why-build-on-roblox]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ip-licensing) To use licensed IP, find it in the Licenses catalog, click Request for License, pick your game, review terms and content standards, include a pitch, and submit. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs ip-licensing|Docs: ip-licensing]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/ip-guidelines) Link a verified email to your account so you receive copyright and trademark removal notifications promptly. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production publishing ip-guidelines|Docs: production / publishing / ip-guidelines]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/packages) Set package Ownership to the group you have permission to edit; ownership transfers are not supported by the asset system, so choose the owner carefully. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects assets packages|Docs: projects / assets / packages]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/activity-history) Use Activity History (Window menu in Studio, or Creator Hub) to audit who changed what and when across a shared game. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects activity-history|Docs: projects / activity-history]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/activity-history) Filter Activity History by event type or collaborator when the log is large to find specific changes quickly. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects activity-history|Docs: projects / activity-history]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/brand-developer-directory) If working in a group or studio, have the group owner submit the application on behalf of the team; the submitter must be over 18. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs creator-programs brand-developer-directory|Docs: creator-programs / brand-developer-directory]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/support/designing-studio-templates) Publish templates under a group only when multiple developers must edit; groups cost a one-time 100 Robux (about $1.25 USD). ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education support designing-studio-templates|Docs: education / support / designing-studio-templ]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/webhooks/webhook-notifications) For group-owned games, only group owners can configure and receive webhook notifications. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud webhooks webhook-notifications|Docs: cloud / webhooks / webhook-notifications]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/rate-limits) API key limits are shared across all keys owned by the same user or group, so separate keys do not add quota. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud reference rate-limits|Docs: cloud / reference / rate-limits]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/creator-store) Sellers should verify with government ID or age check; phone verification does not count and limits distribution volume. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production creator-store|Docs: production / creator-store]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects) Use group roles to control which members can collaborate; collaborators can edit the same scripts, test locally, then commit to the cloud project. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects|Docs: projects]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/construct-the-house) Set the group as package creator so all members can edit packages and share them across every game the group owns. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs resources the-mystery-of-duvall-drive construct-the-house|Docs: resources / the-mystery-of-duvall-drive / co]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies) Recruit developers with expertise in game design, 3D modeling, scripting, and optimization from the Roblox community. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs get-started strategies|Docs: get-started / strategies]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/privacy) To grant a collaborator access, they must be your friend (individual) or you must hold the permission in the group. Groups: only group owners can enable group-level Asset Privacy. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects assets privacy|Docs: projects / assets / privacy]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/create-teams) Use the built-in Teams service for team sorting; it balances players, groups the leaderboard, tints name colors, and spawns teams at separate locations without extra scripting. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs tutorials curriculums gameplay-scripting create-teams|Docs: tutorials / curriculums / gameplay-scripting]])

## [[development-process-and-speed]]
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs) Stage and test config changes in Studio before publishing to live players. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production configs|Docs: production / configs]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs) Use SetTestingValue to test a value on one server only; it does not affect other servers or live players. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production configs|Docs: production / configs]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/collaboration/collaboration-best-practices) Keep a group to-do list that anyone can add tasks to, so every member has something to work on. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education collaboration collaboration-best-practices|Docs: education / collaboration / collaboration-be]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/android-tv-pilot) Dedicate a team member to hit technical milestones and attend milestone reviews within the 90-day window. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs creator-programs android-tv-pilot|Docs: creator-programs / android-tv-pilot]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/weapons-kit) Use the Weapons Kit prefab weapons from the Toolbox Inventory to build competitive combat quickly instead of coding weapons from scratch. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs resources weapons-kit|Docs: resources / weapons-kit]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/cleanup-and-reset) Check that a winner still exists before reading it (activePlayers[1] may be nil if the player left) and return a fallback string instead of erroring. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education battle-royale-series cleanup-and-reset|Docs: education / battle-royale-series / cleanup-a]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-3) Keep the first area scoped to what can be built in about a week, such as a house with a lawn or a simple forest, then expand later. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education lesson-plans roblox-developer roblox-developer-3|Docs: education / lesson-plans / roblox-developer ]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-3) Sketch the map with simple shapes like circles and squares before opening Studio; detailed drawings aren't needed for pre-production. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education lesson-plans roblox-developer roblox-developer-3|Docs: education / lesson-plans / roblox-developer ]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-3) Keep a reference sheet of variable names (e.g. Gold or Rubies) so renamed stats stay consistent across scripts. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education lesson-plans roblox-developer roblox-developer-3|Docs: education / lesson-plans / roblox-developer ]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/finishing-the-project) Once the core project works, expand with more collectible item types and different upgrade types. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education adventure-game-series finishing-the-project|Docs: education / adventure-game-series / finishin]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies) Follow the basic character creation tutorial with Roblox templates before attempting a custom character from scratch. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs avatar character-bodies|Docs: avatar / character-bodies]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/coding-concept-abstraction) Plan and structure code with abstractions up front; the time investment keeps programs organized and makes later changes (adding items, sales) much faster. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education resources coding-concept-abstraction|Docs: education / resources / coding-concept-abstr]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials) Start with the 'Create your first game' tutorial (3D platformer where coins trade for jump power) to learn core Studio concepts before advanced work. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs tutorials|Docs: tutorials]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/reimport) Reimport memory is local to each device; models imported on another device open the Configure dialog, so set the file path there once per machine. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs art modeling reimport|Docs: art / modeling / reimport]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/create-the-map) Write a short game design document first, covering vision and world sketches, to speed up development and make feedback easier to gather. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education adventure-game-series create-the-map|Docs: education / adventure-game-series / create-t]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/auth/oauth2-registration) Skip the general info and redirect URL steps during development; they are only required before submitting for review. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud auth oauth2-registration|Docs: cloud / auth / oauth2-registration]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/auth/oauth2-registration) Once an app is approved for public mode, it cannot be reverted to private mode; editing a public app requires resubmitting for review. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud auth oauth2-registration|Docs: cloud / auth / oauth2-registration]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/badges) Only 5 badges per game are free per 24-hour GMT period; each extra badge in that window costs 100 Robux, so plan badge creation in batches. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production publishing badges|Docs: production / publishing / badges]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/badges) Reorder up to 50 badges at once via drag-and-drop on the Reorder page; new badges appear at the end of the list by default. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production publishing badges|Docs: production / publishing / badges]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/prototyping) Keep prototypes fast and narrow: hit the key aspects of a feature being tested, not the entire feature. Scope is what separates prototyping from building. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design prototyping|Docs: production / game-design / prototyping]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/prototyping) Use paper prototyping for early exploration; it takes minutes to start versus days or weeks for a virtual environment. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design prototyping|Docs: production / game-design / prototyping]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/prototyping) Prototype in Studio once ideas are settled to catch technical infeasibility early, since paper ideas can be impossible to implement. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design prototyping|Docs: production / game-design / prototyping]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/collect-items) Start with a print statement to confirm Touched fires before writing the harvesting logic. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education adventure-game-series collect-items|Docs: education / adventure-game-series / collect-]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/changelog) Make example season/mission times relative to current time so testing in Studio works regardless of date; use absolute times in production configs. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs resources feature-packages changelog|Docs: resources / feature-packages / changelog]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-events) Create events as private first; switch to public when ready to promote. Private events don't appear on the experience detail page. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion experience-events|Docs: production / promotion / experience-events]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/beginner-landing-page) Download Roblox Studio (free, PC or Mac) to build any Roblox game; a 2-button mouse with scroll wheel is strongly recommended. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education build-it-play-it-island-of-move beginner-landing-page|Docs: education / build-it-play-it-island-of-move ]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/publishing) Plan for up to 24 hours of moderation after upload before the item can be edited and enabled for sale. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs art accessories creating-rigid publishing|Docs: art / accessories / creating-rigid / publish]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/coding/next-steps) After variables, learn script types/locations, services, properties and attributes, and events before building custom gameplay behavior. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs tutorials curriculums coding next-steps|Docs: tutorials / curriculums / coding / next-step]])

([[2026-10-28 - Docs resources the-mystery-of-duvall-drive construct-the-house|Docs: resources / the-mystery-of-duvall-drive / co]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/artist/test-and-save) Publish to Roblox every ten minutes or after a big change to avoid losing edits if Studio closes. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs tutorials curriculums artist test-and-save|Docs: tutorials / curriculums / artist / test-and-]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/foundational-gameplay-systems) Ship debug cheat buttons for mission completion during development and strip debug objects in the release build. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs resources the-mystery-of-duvall-drive foundational-gameplay-syste|Docs: resources / the-mystery-of-duvall-drive / fo]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/frequently-asked-questions-education) Roblox Studio requires a PC or Mac; it cannot run on Chromebooks or phones. Use a mouse with a scroll wheel for easier building. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education resources frequently-asked-questions-education|Docs: education / resources / frequently-asked-que]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-2) When stuck, redo the last few steps first, then ask a peer, before asking the teacher (Ask 3 Before Me). ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education lesson-plans roblox-developer roblox-developer-2|Docs: education / lesson-plans / roblox-developer ]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core) Learn the manual steps at least once to understand how Studio works, then use Assistant to speed up repetitive work. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs tutorials curriculums core|Docs: tutorials / curriculums / core]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies) Build reusable systems (quest, collection, event frameworks) to cut future update cost and time. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs get-started strategies|Docs: get-started / strategies]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies) Build a minimal MVP that demonstrates the core loop, then get it to players early to iterate and avoid late pivots. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs get-started strategies|Docs: get-started / strategies]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/procedural-models) Use parameter-driven generators to iterate non-destructively: change attributes like StepSize instead of rebuilding models by hand. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs parts procedural-models|Docs: parts / procedural-models]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/avatar-settings) Use a single source of truth for Avatar Settings across teams and place files, since multi-place universes can cause hard-to-debug avatar behaviour. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs studio avatar-settings|Docs: studio / avatar-settings]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/opening-the-template) Start from the built-in Move It Simulator template, which includes simulator code and a starter world, then customize it rather than building from scratch. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education build-it-play-it-island-of-move opening-the-template|Docs: education / build-it-play-it-island-of-move ]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/in-experience-creation-makeup) Only attempt in-experience makeup if you're comfortable with scripting and Studio; the docs flag it as an advanced workflow. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs avatar in-experience-creation-makeup|Docs: avatar / in-experience-creation-makeup]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences) Plan a beta for 7 to 30 days, long enough to measure D1 and D30 retention. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education developer beta-testing-experiences|Docs: education / developer / beta-testing-experie]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/intro-to-game-and-coding) Separate phases: build first, then code, then polish/playtest, so students don't switch tasks mid-coding. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education lesson-plans intro-to-game-and-coding|Docs: education / lesson-plans / intro-to-game-and]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/manager) Organize assets into folders early; folders hold up to 20,000 assets each and nest up to 20 levels deep. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects assets manager|Docs: projects / assets / manager]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/manager) Use Copy Asset ID to paste IDs directly into AudioPlayer.AssetId or MeshPart.MeshId properties instead of retyping them. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects assets manager|Docs: projects / assets / manager]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/manager) Use the search query language: quotes for exact phrases, -term to exclude, term? to boost, and asset_id to pin specific assets. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects assets manager|Docs: projects / assets / manager]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/manager) Imported assets go through moderation before joining the owning user or group inventory, so import well before you need them. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects assets manager|Docs: projects / assets / manager]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/manager) Drag-and-drop and the Move dialog don't work across inventories or on search results; navigate to the folder first. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects assets manager|Docs: projects / assets / manager]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/localization) Set the source language first (defaults to account language, one at a time) and then add supported languages before starting localization. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production localization|Docs: production / localization]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive) Use Packages to reuse common systems and streamline repeated processes across a project. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs resources the-mystery-of-duvall-drive|Docs: resources / the-mystery-of-duvall-drive]])

## [[common-mistakes]]
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs) Configs do nothing unless your code reads them; call GetValue in the code that uses the value. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production configs|Docs: production / configs]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs) Conditional values must match the default value's data type, and rule order is global; verify priority on the Conditions tab before publishing. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production configs|Docs: production / configs]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs) Use GetConfigForPlayerAsync for targeted configs; GetConfigAsync does not apply targeting. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production configs|Docs: production / configs]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/collaboration/collaboration-best-practices) Ask permission before using someone else's work; never vandalise or put down others' builds. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education collaboration collaboration-best-practices|Docs: education / collaboration / collaboration-be]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/cleanup-and-reset) Map each end state (winner found, timer up, unknown) to its own message branch with an else fallback, so an unhandled state shows an error rather than failing silently. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education battle-royale-series cleanup-and-reset|Docs: education / battle-royale-series / cleanup-a]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies) Do not skip the modesty layer over sensitive body regions when texturing; it is required by Community Standards. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs avatar character-bodies|Docs: avatar / character-bodies]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/reimport) Renaming a mesh in Blender (e.g. Mesh1 to Mesh2) creates a new MeshPart and leaves the original unchanged; plan renames carefully. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs art modeling reimport|Docs: art / modeling / reimport]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/platform-integrated-chat) Platform-integrated chat becomes required for all games from May 2027; unsupported chat APIs will be deprecated, so plan migration before then. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs chat platform-integrated-chat|Docs: chat / platform-integrated-chat]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/platform-integrated-chat) Prefixes aren't supported under platform-integrated chat; if your game requires chat prefixes, don't enable it yet. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs chat platform-integrated-chat|Docs: chat / platform-integrated-chat]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/platform-integrated-chat) Disable the property (PlatformIntegratedChat) again to revert; it can only be changed in Studio, not from scripts. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs chat platform-integrated-chat|Docs: chat / platform-integrated-chat]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/platform-integrated-chat) Games using default Roblox chat with no customizations need no action and should see no change. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs chat platform-integrated-chat|Docs: chat / platform-integrated-chat]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/platform-integrated-chat) Signs, boards, notes and comment systems are not affected by this update; it covers TextChatService only. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs chat platform-integrated-chat|Docs: chat / platform-integrated-chat]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/automatic-skinning-transfer) Do not auto-skin hats, hair or glasses; set AutoSkin to Disabled since they should stay rigid and skinning introduces unwanted deformation. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs avatar automatic-skinning-transfer|Docs: avatar / automatic-skinning-transfer]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/thumbnails) Avoid ambiguous or unclear imagery in thumbnails; it causes player confusion and fewer clicks. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production publishing thumbnails|Docs: production / publishing / thumbnails]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/badges) Disabled badges are hidden from the game page and cannot be earned, so check Badge is Enabled before players are expected to earn it. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production publishing badges|Docs: production / publishing / badges]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/prototyping) Test edge cases where players push the limits of the game, and design prevention or stop-gap solutions, or deliberately encourage the behaviour. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design prototyping|Docs: production / game-design / prototyping]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/prototyping) Do not trust paper prototypes for fun alone: some activities feel more engaging with physical assets than on screen (false positives). ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design prototyping|Docs: production / game-design / prototyping]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/collect-items) Ensure CanHarvest is a child of the touched part; the script only works if the flag sits directly under that part. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education adventure-game-series collect-items|Docs: education / adventure-game-series / collect-]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/collect-items) Put the tool in StarterPack, not Workspace, or it will not be equipped on spawn. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education adventure-game-series collect-items|Docs: education / adventure-game-series / collect-]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/collect-items) Use canHarvest.Value, not canHarvest = true; spell Transparency and CanCollide exactly. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education adventure-game-series collect-items|Docs: education / adventure-game-series / collect-]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/collect-items) The leaderstats names in FindFirstChild must match the IntValue names used in PlayerSetup exactly. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education adventure-game-series collect-items|Docs: education / adventure-game-series / collect-]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wonder/connect-the-beam) If a beam's arrow points backwards, check that Attachment0 is on the player and Attachment1 on the goal, or mirror custom textures. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wonder connect-the-beam|Docs: education / build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wond]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices) Do not make the inner and outer cage identical; the system cannot determine the accessory and deformation breaks. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs avatar layered-accessories caging-best-practices|Docs: avatar / layered-accessories / caging-best-p]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices) Do not place clothing inside the inner cage; it causes artifacts when layered over other clothing. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs avatar layered-accessories caging-best-practices|Docs: avatar / layered-accessories / caging-best-p]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices) Do not let the outer cage sit inside the inner cage or leave cages missing heads or limbs; these are rejected during validation. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs avatar layered-accessories caging-best-practices|Docs: avatar / layered-accessories / caging-best-p]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices) Avoid collapsed or overlapping cage vertices; they cause artifacts and are rejected during validation. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs avatar layered-accessories caging-best-practices|Docs: avatar / layered-accessories / caging-best-p]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices) Keep the clothing mesh inside the outer cage; mesh outside the outer cage does not deform and is rejected during validation. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs avatar layered-accessories caging-best-practices|Docs: avatar / layered-accessories / caging-best-p]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices) Avoid oversized gaps between clothing and outer cage; they cause severe deformation when combined with other layers. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs avatar layered-accessories caging-best-practices|Docs: avatar / layered-accessories / caging-best-p]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/changelog) Do not rely on server-side jump detection; it is inconsistent, so the example package removed it. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs resources feature-packages changelog|Docs: resources / feature-packages / changelog]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/classic-clothing) Many user-generated avatars on the Marketplace do not support 2D classic clothing; check for modern 3D cosmetic options instead for rigid accessories. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs avatar classic-clothing|Docs: avatar / classic-clothing]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/beginner-landing-page) Never share your Roblox password, even with real-life friends; use a password that is not guessable from your username. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education build-it-play-it-island-of-move beginner-landing-page|Docs: education / build-it-play-it-island-of-move ]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/beginner-landing-page) Roblox employees never ask for passwords; report anyone who does via Report Abuse. There is no free Robux; ignore sites or players claiming otherwise. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education build-it-play-it-island-of-move beginner-landing-page|Docs: education / build-it-play-it-island-of-move ]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/dsa-ip-reporting) Link a verified email to your Roblox account; Roblox notifies you by in-platform message and email when content is removed or an account is suspended under the IP Policy. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production publishing dsa-ip-reporting|Docs: production / publishing / dsa-ip-reporting]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/advertise-on-roblox) Violating advertising standards can get your game or account suspended; check content against the questions in the docs before publishing. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion advertise-on-roblox|Docs: production / promotion / advertise-on-roblox]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads) Do not manipulate impressions or teleports; Roblox may deduct or reclaim Robux, and may suspend the game or account for fraud. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production monetization immersive-ads|Docs: production / monetization / immersive-ads]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion) All promotional content must follow Community Standards and Terms of Use; advertising must also follow Advertising Standards. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion|Docs: production / promotion]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets) Do not expect rbxgameasset names to load when pasting objects into another game; they will fail or load a different same-named asset. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects assets|Docs: projects / assets]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/collaboration) Before reverting a place version, disable Team Create; otherwise other editors' auto-saves can overwrite the revert. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects collaboration|Docs: projects / collaboration]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/module) Avoid ModuleScripts requiring each other in a circle; this causes a 'Requested module was required recursively' error. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs scripting module|Docs: scripting / module]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/roblox-plus) Only the server owner's time counts for paid private server payouts; time spent by invited subscribers does not count toward your earnings. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production monetization roblox-plus|Docs: production / monetization / roblox-plus]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/monetization) Diagnose low conversion: check for expensive items, poor shop visibility, unappealing content, and confusing purchase flow steps. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production analytics monetization|Docs: production / analytics / monetization]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/main-page) Never share your Roblox password, make it hard to guess, and never trust anyone claiming free Robux or a secret method to get it. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway main-page|Docs: education / build-it-play-it-galactic-speedw]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/add-rounds) Do not rely on the default Teams scoring for round-based games, since it ties team score to current players and drops points when a scorer disconnects. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs tutorials curriculums gameplay-scripting add-rounds|Docs: tutorials / curriculums / gameplay-scripting]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/explorer) When searching for names with spaces, wrap the full name in double quotes (e.g. tag:"Light Source") or the search logic treats spaces as a combined query. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs studio explorer|Docs: studio / explorer]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/support/roblox-account-creation) Distrust anyone claiming secret methods for free Robux; never trust such players or sites. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education support roblox-account-creation|Docs: education / support / roblox-account-creatio]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/errors) Don't assume one error shape: v1 endpoints return three different formats, so parse both 'error' and 'code' keys and the validation-style 'errors' object. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud reference errors|Docs: cloud / reference / errors]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/examples/rate-limit-public-text-inputs) All text shown to other players must pass through the text filter (GetNonChatStringForBroadcastAsync) before broadcasting; wrap the call in pcall. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs chat examples rate-limit-public-text-inputs|Docs: chat / examples / rate-limit-public-text-inp]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace) Do not confuse the Marketplace (avatar items for sale) with the Creator Store (models, images, plugins for building games). ([[2026-10-28 - Docs marketplace|Docs: marketplace]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/develop-a-moving-world) Beams cannot be synced to audio via particle systems alone; script the timing when no sequencer exists in Studio. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs resources the-mystery-of-duvall-drive develop-a-moving-world|Docs: resources / the-mystery-of-duvall-drive / de]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/test-blender) Destructive modeling changes or overridden saved animations can corrupt facial or skinning data; reapplying steps or restarting from the original template may be faster than repairing. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs avatar character-bodies test-blender|Docs: avatar / character-bodies / test-blender]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/creating) Don't assume game-making is too hard; the docs frame it as approachable, with tutorials walking through building and scripting basics. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs get-started creating|Docs: get-started / creating]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-2/parameters-practice-buttons) If a bridge is solid at game start, check that its parts are anchored and not touching terrain or other parts, which can fire Touched early. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs tutorials fundamentals coding-2 parameters-practice-buttons|Docs: tutorials / fundamentals / coding-2 / parame]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines) Do not add 17+ content before generating a 17+ age recommendation; the questionnaire must be completed first or the experience risks moderation. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion experience-guidelines|Docs: production / promotion / experience-guidelin]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines) Base questionnaire answers on the most mature or extreme content players can encounter, not the typical content. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion experience-guidelines|Docs: production / promotion / experience-guidelin]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines) Any implied violence (e.g. off-screen sounds) automatically counts as mild; any realistic consequence of violence, even once, counts as moderate or strong. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion experience-guidelines|Docs: production / promotion / experience-guidelin]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines) Any depicted blood, even from a distance, counts as light; pools, gushing, organs, dismemberment or up-close spatter count as heavy (17+ only). ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion experience-guidelines|Docs: production / promotion / experience-guidelin]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines) Experience guidelines don't cover user-generated avatar items players bring in; only content you create counts. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion experience-guidelines|Docs: production / promotion / experience-guidelin]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines) Inaccurate guidelines can lead to guideline removal, restriction to 13+ visibility, and moderation actions; misrepresentation may bring consequences. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion experience-guidelines|Docs: production / promotion / experience-guidelin]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines) Free-form user creation and social hangouts are restricted to players 13+ regardless of parental controls. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion experience-guidelines|Docs: production / promotion / experience-guidelin]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/data-model) Do not add Player objects to the Players container manually; the engine creates them automatically on join. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects data-model|Docs: projects / data-model]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/data-model) Name objects descriptively so they are easy to find and modify later. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects data-model|Docs: projects / data-model]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/introduction-to-quest-design) Avoid scarce or overly effort-intensive reward sources; they discourage economy participation and create perceptions of unfairness. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production game-design introduction-to-quest-design|Docs: production / game-design / introduction-to-q]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-notifications) Avoid purely informational notifications; prompt an immediate action like rejoining or responding to a challenge. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs production promotion experience-notifications|Docs: production / promotion / experience-notifica]])

- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/debugging) Use breakpoints as the primary tool for debugging functions; they let you pause and inspect variables, watch values and view the call stack. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs studio debugging|Docs: studio / debugging]])

## [[age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]]
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies) Ensure all avatar assets follow Roblox Community Standards and applicable Marketplace policies. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs avatar character-bodies|Docs: avatar / character-bodies]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/auth/oauth2-registration) Demo video for review must be public, under 1 minute, start with the user signed out, and show the full authorization flow back to the app. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud auth oauth2-registration|Docs: cloud / auth / oauth2-registration]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/auth/oauth2-registration) Select the single app category that matches your app; the scope set must fall under one category in the Creator Third-Party App Policy. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud auth oauth2-registration|Docs: cloud / auth / oauth2-registration]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/frequently-asked-questions) Roblox may temporarily or permanently revoke creation access for policy violations; appeals go through the appeals page. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs marketplace frequently-asked-questions|Docs: marketplace / frequently-asked-questions]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/frequently-asked-questions) For non-IP violations of Community Standards on avatar items, use Report Abuse on the item's catalog page or submit a support ticket; EU residents use the Illegal Content Reporting Form (DSA). ([[2026-10-28 - Docs marketplace frequently-asked-questions|Docs: marketplace / frequently-asked-questions]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/learn-explore-sort) Keep content appropriate for a classroom of students of all ages, even when subject matter is advanced. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs creator-programs learn-explore-sort|Docs: creator-programs / learn-explore-sort]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players) State game rules clearly where all users can see them, apply them fairly, and ensure rules comply with Community Standards. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs players|Docs: players]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players) Ban messages may point to off-platform contact by brand or platform name, but must not include usernames, handles, or direct links. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs players|Docs: players]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/usage-messaging) Combine published messages with DataStore blocklists in a moderation system so banned users cannot rejoin. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides usage-messaging|Docs: cloud / guides / usage-messaging]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/profile-card) Players under 13 only see status messages that are appropriate for their age group; do not rely on status text being visible to all players. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs resources modules profile-card|Docs: resources / modules / profile-card]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/audio/objects) All TTS text and STT audio must adhere to Roblox Community Standards and Terms of Use. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs audio objects|Docs: audio / objects]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/video-frames) Only upload videos you have legal rights to use and that meet Roblox Community Standards and Terms of Use; non-compliant uploads are rejected. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs ui video-frames|Docs: ui / video-frames]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/marketplace-policy) Verify government ID (or link a verified parental account) before uploading or publishing; 2-step verification is also required to publish. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs marketplace marketplace-policy|Docs: marketplace / marketplace-policy]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/marketplace-policy) Avoid sexual, political, religious, gory, violent, drug or self-harm themed items, and items likely used for such roleplay; avoid excessive text. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs marketplace marketplace-policy|Docs: marketplace / marketplace-policy]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/guidelines) Run all incoming user-originated text through text filtering before displaying it to other users. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs chat guidelines|Docs: chat / guidelines]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/guidelines) Call TextChannel:SendAsync() for messages, which handles basic privacy and parental settings automatically. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs chat guidelines|Docs: chat / guidelines]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/guidelines) Call TextChannel:SetDirectChatRequester() on TextChannels created for direct chat so they are marked correctly. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs chat guidelines|Docs: chat / guidelines]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/guidelines) Check TextChatService:CanUserChatAsync(), CanUsersChatAsync() or CanUsersDirectChatAsync() before allowing messaging requests. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs chat guidelines|Docs: chat / guidelines]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/guidelines) Use TextChannels for chat so messages stay reportable for abuse and visible to moderators; they handle this automatically. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs chat guidelines|Docs: chat / guidelines]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/guidelines) Repeated community-standard violations lead to warnings and temporary chat restrictions; see Text Chat Nudge FAQ. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs chat guidelines|Docs: chat / guidelines]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar-setup) Expect imported models to enter the moderation queue; appeals can take up to 10 minutes to resolve. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs avatar-setup|Docs: avatar-setup]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/animate-in-roblox-lesson) If using motion-based mobile play or physical reference poses, set strict safety guidelines and a safe space for movement. - 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/support/private-servers-for-classroom-use) Teach students to use in-game reporting tools before starting online play, and how to react to bullies and angry players. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education support private-servers-for-classroom-use|Docs: education / support / private-servers-for-cl]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/group-guidelines) Ensure all group-uploaded assets, avatars and games follow Roblox Community Standards and Terms of Use; these apply to groups too. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects group-guidelines|Docs: projects / group-guidelines]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/group-guidelines) As group owner, make reasonable effort to address harmful behavior and proactively moderate using group audit logs. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects group-guidelines|Docs: projects / group-guidelines]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/group-guidelines) Do not use your group to promote or upload violative content; this can lead to enforcement on your account and permanent group deletion. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs projects group-guidelines|Docs: projects / group-guidelines]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/brand-developer-directory) Keep account moderation history in good standing and meet Community Standards; these are required for directory eligibility. - 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/preset-system-guidelines) Presets that enable dynamic or directed conversation require users to be age checked (Facial Age Estimation or ID verification) and of similar age to chat. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs chat preset-system-guidelines|Docs: chat / preset-system-guidelines]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/preset-system-guidelines) Presets limited to gameplay actions, confirmations, or basic reactions can be used regardless of age or age-check status. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs chat preset-system-guidelines|Docs: chat / preset-system-guidelines]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/preset-system-guidelines) Run every preset through Class.TextService:FilterStringAsync() before sending. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs chat preset-system-guidelines|Docs: chat / preset-system-guidelines]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/dynamic-heads) Ensure all created assets follow Roblox Community Standards and applicable Marketplace policies. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs avatar dynamic-heads|Docs: avatar / dynamic-heads]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/moderation) Appeal incorrect moderation decisions with the moderation staff; use the IP infringement notice form for IP-related removals. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs marketplace moderation|Docs: marketplace / moderation]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/moderation) Archiving does not replace IP vigilance; an archived item can still be subject to an IP takedown, and archiving is considered in account penalties. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs marketplace moderation|Docs: marketplace / moderation]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wonder/beginner-landing-page) Report anyone asking for your password using the Report Abuse feature; Roblox employees never ask for passwords. - 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wonder/beginner-landing-page) Distrust anyone offering free Robux; there is no legitimate free Robux source. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wonder beginner-landing-pag|Docs: education / build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wond]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/text-input) Apply text filtering to any user-entered text you display; Roblox is not responsible for it and will remove games that don't filter. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs ui text-input|Docs: ui / text-input]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/accelerated-workflows) You are responsible for content generated in your games; follow Community Standards, since all generations pass moderation. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs ai accelerated-workflows|Docs: ai / accelerated-workflows]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/accelerated-workflows) Manage your data sharing preferences for AI training on Creator Hub. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs ai accelerated-workflows|Docs: ai / accelerated-workflows]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/intellectual-property) Only upload content you own or have the owner's permission to use; infringement violates both Roblox Terms of Use and the law. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs marketplace intellectual-property|Docs: marketplace / intellectual-property]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/intellectual-property) Infringing items are removed and repeat infringers terminated; creators lose Limited per-unit fees and Robux earned from sales of the removed item. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs marketplace intellectual-property|Docs: marketplace / intellectual-property]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/intellectual-property) Only the rights holder or their authorized representative can file a takedown report; reports from third parties cannot be acted on. - 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/educator-onboarding/3-setting-up-a-roblox-classroom) Students who list an age under 13 see only curated games deemed appropriate by Roblox; account restrictions apply. - 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/in-experience-creation-makeup) Listen to AvatarAssetModerationCompleted per asset and AvatarOutfitModerationCompleted for the whole outfit before using creations in-experience. - 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/best-practices) Configure automated right-to-be-forgotten processing for player data following static key patterns; use the right-to-erasure webhook only if your schema isn't supported, and verify all matching data is removed. ## [[ab-testing-and-experiments]]
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/configs) Publish with GradualRollout (15-minute rollout) instead of Immediate when changing live gameplay values, to limit blast radius. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides configs|Docs: cloud / guides / configs]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/experiments) Exactly one variant must have isBaseline true; the productType discriminator must match the populated configuration block (inGameConfigExperimentConfiguration or matchmakingExperimentConfiguration). ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides experiments|Docs: cloud / guides / experiments]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/experiments) Experiment configuration becomes immutable once started or scheduled; edit duration, rollout and variants while still in draft. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides experiments|Docs: cloud / guides / experiments]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/experiments) Wait at least 24 hours after starting an experiment before pulling /stats diagnostics. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides experiments|Docs: cloud / guides / experiments]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/experiments) Check isSrmDetected in stats; a true value signals sample ratio mismatch, so the split may be unreliable. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides experiments|Docs: cloud / guides / experiments]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/experiments) Omit variantId when calling :complete to stop without a winner; the config reverts to its previous value. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides experiments|Docs: cloud / guides / experiments]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/experiments) Pick a goal metric matching the hypothesis, e.g. DAY_1_RETENTION for a boss-health change, and keep the test tied to it. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides experiments|Docs: cloud / guides / experiments]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores-vs-memory-stores) Use configs for feature flags and tunable values (enemy health, weapon damage, item prices, event toggles) so you can change them live without restarting servers. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud-services data-stores-vs-memory-stores|Docs: cloud-services / data-stores-vs-memory-store]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/metrics) Use ThumbnailWinningSegments to see which audience segments a thumbnail performs best with before choosing a creative. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud guides analytics metrics|Docs: cloud / guides / analytics / metrics]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/planning-for-educational-settings) Test games on low-end devices such as Chromebooks, not just high-end PCs, since classrooms commonly use them. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs education developer planning-for-educational-settings|Docs: education / developer / planning-for-educati]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/right-to-be-forgotten) Publishing a config draft is irreversible except by restoring a previous version; verify the draft with GET before POST publish. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs cloud-services data-stores right-to-be-forgotten|Docs: cloud-services / data-stores / right-to-be-f]])
- 2026-10-28 [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/artist/test-and-save) Playtest often using Test or Test Here so your avatar spawns; Run skips avatar insertion, which blocks testing in-game VFX interactions. ([[2026-10-28 - Docs tutorials curriculums artist test-and-save|Docs: tutorials / curriculums / artist / test-and-]])
